Experiment 5 showing existing results is odd since we just launched it — the log file probably contains leftover data from a previous run, possibly because the old tmux session wasn't fully cleaned up or the append mode carried over old entries.

Looking at the process IDs and timestamps, the old session had PID 362915 while the new one is writing fresh output. The `tee` command should overwrite by default, but I'm seeing task progress that could be from either the old or new run.
